## Missing-slug notes

- `meharg2008-rice-arsenic-literature-review`: taxonomy has rice-grain, rice-milk, rice-based cereal/snack, mixed-meal, and infant-food rows, but no broad unresolved infant-formula row for the report's unspecified infant-formula groups. Formula means from Table 3.11 are retained in Key numbers but not routed to a formula product row.
- `rokanuzzaman2022-bangladesh-eggs-metals`: taxonomy lacks exact duck-egg and quail-egg ingredient/product slugs. Frontmatter routes through broad `eggs`, `chicken`, `poultry`, `eggs-product`, and `poultry-product`; duck/quail remain matrix descriptors and source text.
- `buba2020-domestic-pig-metals-nigeria`: taxonomy lacks exact pork kidney, pork liver, and pork muscle ingredient/product slugs. Frontmatter routes through broad `meat`, `organ-meats`, and `pork-product`; tissue forms remain matrix descriptors and source text.
- `faraj2023-backyard-chicken-metals-iraq`: taxonomy lacks exact backyard-chicken-breast and backyard-chicken-liver ingredient/product slugs. Frontmatter routes through broad `chicken`, `poultry`, `meat`, `organ-meats`, and `poultry-product`.
- `khalafalla2011-beef-carcasses-metals-egypt`: taxonomy lacks exact beef kidney, beef liver, and beef muscle ingredient/product slugs. Frontmatter routes through broad `beef`, `meat`, `organ-meats`, and `beef-product`.
- `edqm2024-metals-alloys-food-contact-guide`: taxonomy has no `metals/zirconium` page slug. The Zr SRL is retained in the source frontmatter and Key numbers, but no zirconium wiki-page link was invented.
- `thomas2022-lead-game-meat-awareness`: taxonomy has a `game-meats` product slug but no exact game-meat ingredient slug. Frontmatter routes through broad `meat` and `meat-and-poultry`, with `wild-game-meat`, `venison`, and `gamebird-meat` kept as matrix descriptors.
- `hunt2009-lead-bullet-fragments-venison`: taxonomy has a `game-meats` product slug but no exact venison/game-meat ingredient slug. Frontmatter routes through broad `meat` and `meat-and-poultry`, with `venison`, `game-meat`, `ground-meat`, and `lead-ammunition` kept as matrix descriptors.
- `qvarfort2025-game-meat-lead-bioaccessibility`: taxonomy has a `game-meats` product slug but no exact wild-boar-meat/game-meat ingredient slug. Frontmatter routes through broad `meat`, `meat-and-poultry`, and `organ-meats`, with exact sample locations retained as matrix descriptors and source text.
- `morshdy2023-canned-meat-chicken-metals`: taxonomy lacks exact canned-beef, canned-corned-beef, and chicken-luncheon product slugs. Frontmatter routes through broad `processed-meats`, `beef-product`, and `poultry-product`, with exact product forms retained as matrix descriptors.
- `campaignsafecosmetics2007-lead-lipstick`: taxonomy contains `lipstick`, but the page is retired pending a future adult Cat 13 lip-cosmetics row. The source uses the closed `lipstick` slug and records the non-retired adult-lip-cosmetics destination gap; no new slug was invented.
- `zafarzadeh2018-cosmetics-lead-cadmium-iran`: taxonomy lacks a non-retired adult lip-products slug, exact eye-makeup-remover slug, and exact pancake-makeup slug. Frontmatter uses `lipstick` for closed-vocabulary continuity, `facial-cleansers` for eye makeup remover, and `makeup-foundation-powders-blush` for pancake/foundation-type makeup.
- `yorgwara2026-personal-care-metals-nigeria`: taxonomy lacks a non-retired adult lip-products slug, adult bar-soap/bathing-soap slug, facial-powder-only slug, and exact hair-food slug. Frontmatter uses broad available products (`lipstick`, `body-wash-shower-gel`, `makeup-foundation-powders-blush`, and `leave-on-hair-preparations`) and does not invent new slugs.
- `yashim2020-hair-dyes-metals-nigeria`: taxonomy lacks a precise adult dye-shampoo slug. Frontmatter routes broadly through `hair-dye-rinse-off`, `permanent-hair-dyes-tints`, and `shampoo-adult`.
- `gautam2023-tattoo-ink-heavy-metals-risk`: no missing product or ingredient slug blockers. The exact `tattoo-inks` product slug exists; arsenic and mercury are routed as total species because the report does not provide iAs or MeHg.
- `modddaresi2022-cosmetics-heavy-metal-limits`: no missing product or ingredient slug blockers. `products` is intentionally empty because the source is a general regulatory/method review without routeable product occurrence values.

Frontmatter uses broad available products (`leave-on-preparations-other` and `face-neck-leave-on-skin-care`) and keeps `lipstick`, `skin-lightening-cream`, and `whitening-products` for exact routes.
- `piccinini2016-tattoo-pmu-safety`: taxonomy has `tattoo-inks` but no distinct permanent-make-up-ink product slug. PMU specificity is retained in matrices/body text. Selenium appears in the source comparator and `metals: [Se]`, but the current metal page taxonomy has no `[[metals/selenium]]` page, so no selenium wiki link was added.

- Nonblocking check/build warnings observed: DOI audit reported `4` peer-reviewed/study pages missing DOI metadata and `542` DOI-bearing pages missing `access_url`; index-fanning audit reported `8` product-slug leaks; routing-discipline audit was run in `--warn-only` mode and reported `12` missing-product failures plus `22` zero-routed HMTc-locked products; Quartz warned that concurrently generated untracked dossier pages have inaccurate git dates; KaTeX/LaTeX strict mode warned on dash Unicode in warn mode.

## Concurrency notes

- Shared tree is dirty from concurrent FRANK/autonomy/audit work. Commits are scoped by explicit pathspec; generated/concurrent dirty files are left unstaged unless directly generated for the current source.
- Concurrent audit commit `b804e42` swept the generated Woreta route row into `data/evidence/product_source_routing_audit.csv` while the source page was still staged locally; the Woreta source-page commit `f15cb50` therefore contains the page and audit rows, while the route row is already present in HEAD.
- The pre-commit route rebuild for `zhang2022-pork-heavy-metals-china` also staged one unrelated generated `routing_malformed.csv` advisory for `jaafar2020-pediococcus-lead-cadmium-bioremediation`; the pork source itself is absent from unresolved/malformed routing files.
